### Env-switch audit — every switch proved live on the binary (PERF_METHOD §5)

`envaudit_v3.sh`: for each switch, one 12-frame profiled run at the default and one at the rollback
value (nsys `cuda_gpu_kern_sum` for the kernel names and template arguments actually launched; the
sqlite trace where a switch changes grid, stream or issue order rather than the kernel). Fresh
prefix per run, the script fails if no csv or sqlite appears. `audit_report.txt` is the full
reading; the table is the verdict per switch.

| switch (step) | default → what the binary launches | rollback → what the binary launches | live? |
|---|---|---|---|
| `UIPC_DSB_GAUSS_NEWTON` (s02) | `DiscreteShellBending_do_compute_gradient_hessian_kernel<3, 1>`, 90 launches, 351 µs | `=0` → `<1, 1>`, 89 launches, **3483 µs** | **yes** |
| `UIPC_CONTACT_RANK1` (s07/V2) | contact part 2 `do_assemble_kernel<0, 2, 0, 0, 1, **1**>` (PE+PP rank-1, the V2 default), 318 µs | `=0` → `<0, 2, 0, 0, 1, **0**>` exact, 840 µs; part 1 unchanged `<…, 8>` | **yes** |
| `UIPC_CONTACT_SPD1_BASIS` (s08) | contact part 1 `do_assemble_kernel<0, 1, 1, 1, 0, **8**>` (basis-free), 1456 µs | `=0` → `<0, 1, 1, 1, 0, **1**>` (dense-Q eigen path), 1685 µs; part 2 unchanged `<…, 1>` | **yes** |
| `UIPC_CCD_EARLY_OUT` (s04) | `filter_toi_k3/k4_kernel<**1**, 0, 1>`, k4 801 µs | `=0` → `<**0**, 0, 1>`, k4 **1049 µs** (+31 %) | **yes** |
| `UIPC_CCD_COMPACT` (s06) | `filter_toi_k*<1, 0, **1**>`; `filter_active_k4` 55 µs; four `DeviceSelectSweep` at 3–5 µs | `=0` → `<1, 0, **0**>`; `filter_active_k4` **407 µs**, `k3` 232 µs; the selects at 10–20 µs (raw candidate arrays) | **yes** |
| `UIPC_CONTACT_SPLIT` (s09/K9), **`UIPC_ABD_GH_PREPASS=1` pinned in all three arms** | `=2`: part 1 `<0, **1**, …>` ×19 on **stream 14**, part 2 `<0, **2**, …>` ×19 on stream 7 | `=1` → the same two instantiations, **103 launches all on stream 7**, no stream 14; `=0` → one fused `<0, **0**, 1, 1, 1, 1>` ×19, no part 1/2 instantiation at all | **yes** (and s11's warning stands: without the pin, `=0`/`=1` also silently move the prepass to mode 1) |
| `UIPC_ABD_GH_PREPASS` (s10/s11) | `=4` (default): `affine_body_bdf1_kinetic_…` and `ortho_potential_compute_gradient_hessian_kernel<1>` ×28 each on **stream 15**; **18 of 28** ortho launches start *inside* a contact part-1 window and run 593 µs (stretched by sharing SMs — s10's mechanism) | `=1` → same kernels on stream 15, **0 of 28** inside part 1, 343 µs; `=0` → **stream 7** (the default stream), stream 15 gone, 307 µs | **yes**, and the three modes are distinguishable at kernel level by the placement signature, not just the stream |
| `UIPC_CONTACT_DEFERRED_JOIN` (s14) | `=1`: on case2, **296** non-contact default-stream kernels (SNH G/H, BDF1 kinetic, strain limiting, fills) start *inside* a contact part-1 window across 38 windows | `=0` → **0** | **yes** |
| `UIPC_SPMV_GRID_FIT` (s13) | `Spmv_rbk_sym_spmv_dot_chunked_kernel` gridX **576**, 600 launches, 58.8 µs (mas-bunny) | `=0` → gridX **3518** (the capacity grid), 60.5 µs | **yes** |
| `UIPC_MAS_ROWDOT2` (s15) | `schwarz_local_solve_fused_R_kernel<1,1>` + `<0,0>` (see s16) | `=0` → `schwarz_local_solve_rowdot_kernel` (round-4) **and** `build_multi_level_R_kernel`, 624 each | **yes — but note the coupling below** |
| `UIPC_MAS_FUSED_R` (s16) | `fused_R_kernel<1,1>` 27.4 µs + `<0,0>` 4.6 µs, no `build_multi_level_R` | `=0` → `rowdot2_kernel<16, 0, 0>` 25.1 µs + `build_multi_level_R_kernel` 15.1 µs | **yes** |
| `UIPC_SEG_REDUCE2` (s17) | `fast_segmental_reduce_matrix_k2_kernel<128,32,double,3,3,…>` 194 µs, `<…,3,1,…>` 30 µs | `=0` → `fast_segmental_reduce_matrix_kernel<…>` 231 / 35 µs | **yes** |

**No dead switch.** Two couplings a future A/B must know about, one already recorded and one new:

- (s11, re-confirmed) `UIPC_CONTACT_SPLIT` measures the split alone only with
  `UIPC_ABD_GH_PREPASS=1` pinned in both arms.
- (**new**) `UIPC_MAS_ROWDOT2=0` **also turns s16 off**: `fused_restriction_active()` requires
  `rowdot2_mode() > 0`, so the `=0` arm launches `rowdot_kernel + build_multi_level_R` — the
  pre-s15 *and* pre-s16 path. It is the correct rollback for both steps together, but an A/B of
  s15 alone is `UIPC_MAS_FUSED_R=0` vs `UIPC_MAS_FUSED_R=0 UIPC_MAS_ROWDOT2=0`, not
  `UIPC_MAS_ROWDOT2=0` vs default. (`UIPC_MAS_FUSED_R=0` alone does isolate s16.)

Also re-confirmed from V1: `UIPC_DSB_OCC` (s01) does not exist on `main`; s01 was rejected and never
merged.

### The round's own verification probes, composed and run together on the head

`probes_v3.sh`, `probes/`. Every probe re-runs the old path beside the new one on device inside the
shipped binary and counts mismatches; run with all twelve steps' defaults in force, so each probe
is also a test of whether a *later* step broke an *earlier* step's verifier (the §5 case round 5 hit).

| probe (step) | scene, frames | result |
|---|---|---|
| `UIPC_DSB_GN_VERIFY=1` (s02) — gradient of the exact vs Gauss-Newton kernel | tumbler 12 / cube-wall 12 | **60 833 920 / 11 235 840 words compared, 0 mismatching** (the tumbler figure is bit-for-bit V1's) |
| `UIPC_CCD_COMPACT_VERIFY=1` (s06) — compacted vs raw active sets | tumbler 40 | 200 calls, 114.6 M candidates, **89.07 % dropped, MISMATCHES=0**, contents checks 200 / 0 |
| `UIPC_ABD_GH_PREPASS_VERIFY=1` (s10) — prepass G/H vs in-place | rwb 12 / cube-wall 12 | **5 014 464 / 19 768 320 words, 0 mismatching**; `abd_gh_prepass_verify_kernel` launched 28 / 33 times (it ran) |
| `UIPC_CONTACT_DEFERRED_JOIN_VERIFY=1` (s14) — collected G/H at the deferred join vs a snapshot at launch | case2 40 / rwb 40 | mode=1, **97.9 % / 99.0 % of joins still pending at host time**, 50.5 M / 166.3 M words, **0 mismatching after the join, 0 stale before it** |
| `UIPC_MAS_APPLY_VERIFY=5` (s16) — fused restriction + local solve vs the two-kernel path | mas-bunny 12 / case2 12 | **`Z_fine` max\|diff\| = 0** (bit-identical) in both; `Z_coarse` worst rel 6.0e-6 / 5.0e-6, `R_coarse` 2.0e-6 / 1.5e-6 — the atomic-accumulation tail at s16's recorded level |
| `UIPC_MAS_R_TAIL_VERIFY=1` (s16) — the coarse R tail is zero at apply entry | mas-bunny 12 | `check_r_tail_zero_kernel` launched 624 times, **0 "R tail not zero"** |
| `UIPC_SEG_VERIFY=1 UIPC_SEG_HIST=1` (s17) — k2 vs old segmented reduce | mas-bunny / case2 / tumbler 12 | 24 / 38 / 224 launches, 27.8 M / 98.0 M / 58.8 M words: **mismatches only in multi-warp slots (`elsewhere=0` on all 286 launches)**, max rel 6.8e-14 / 4.7e-14 / 6.9e-12 — s17's "bit-identical where the old kernel stores, old-path noise where it accumulates" |
| `UIPC_SPMV_VERIFY=1` (s13) — fitted-grid SpMV+dot vs reference | mas-bunny / case2 12 | 555 / 1240 launches, max rel y 3.8e-14 / 1.7e-15, max rel dot 1.7e-13 / 6.0e-15 |

Every probe still works at this head and every one reports what its step recorded. The two
approximations (s02, s07/V2) are by construction not covered by a mismatch probe — their evidence is
V1 and V2, and this pass's iteration counts are consistent with both.

### Limits, stated plainly

- **One box, one GPU, cc 7.5** (RTX 2070 SUPER, FP64 1/32, 8 GB). Round 5's acceptance run showed
  the same changes reading −6.6 to −30.1 % on a 5090 against different local numbers; **no rented
  hardware this round**, so the transfer predictions recorded per step are still predictions. The
  2070S over-rewards the spill/register-driven part of s08/s15 and the occupancy-shaped part of
  s10/s11 (the brief's table); s02, s04, s06, s13, s14, s16, s17 are algorithmic or grid-vs-machine
  and should carry.
- **N runs is N runs.** n = 20 on the tumbler and n = 5 elsewhere; at n = 5 a 5-vs-5 test cannot
  produce p < 0.008 and cannot see a sub-1 % effect — none of the five headline numbers is near
  that limit, but the rwb Newton shift (−2.7 %, p = 0.02) is exactly the kind of n = 5 reading
  §2.4 warns about, and it is reported as such.
- **Base and head ran the head's scene code.** That is the right comparison for the engine, and the
  only scene the base tag lacks is the tumbler itself.
- **The peak-memory statistic is a 0.2-s poll of total device memory.** On the tumbler its per-arm
  range is ±30 %; the number to quote is the envelope (head max 1460 MiB < base median 1541), not
  the mean.
- The switch audit is 12-frame runs: enough to prove which kernel runs, not a performance
  measurement (the per-launch µs in the table are within-run readings on a scene the round has
  said is noisy per launch).
- Sanitizers were run by V1 (memcheck clean, two pre-existing findings reproduced on a baseline
  binary) and not repeated here for s09–s17; the probes above are the device-side evidence for
  those steps.

### Found outside this pass's area (reported, not fixed)

- **`pgrep -f` inside a script hung this pass for 20 minutes**, and the mechanism is reproducible
  (`pgrep_test`): the Claude Code tool shell runs every command as `/bin/bash -c '… eval "<the
  whole command text>"'`, so the *command text itself* — including the contents of any heredoc
  that writes a script — is in a live process's cmdline for as long as that tool call runs. A
  `while pgrep -f 'ab2.py'` guard written by heredoc in a call that then polled for several minutes
  matched the shell that wrote it. VAST_AI.md's `pgrep`/`pkill` self-match note covers the
  interactive form; this is the heredoc form. Don't gate on `pgrep -f` from a script launched by the
  same tool call; gate on a sentinel file or the job's own exit.
- `configure_perf.sh` passes `-DVCPKG_INSTALLED_DIR=…/build-dahl/vcpkg_installed`, but the project's
  `uipc_config_vcpkg_install` overrides it to `<build>/vcpkg_installed` unconditionally, so
  `build-perf` has its own (identical) copy and the flag is decorative. Harmless; worth a comment
  in the script.
- `run_benchmark.py` records `revisions.libuipc` from the *driver* checkout, so a two-build sweep's
  metadata shows the head commit for both arms; `ab2.py` records each arm's native-library md5 and
  interpreter path to disambiguate. A `--label` or an `uipc.__version__`-with-hash would make the
  raw json self-describing.

### Process

Branch `perf/round6-v3-final`, cut from `main` = `6b1e21cb`; this section and the evidence directory
only, no engine source touched; `main` checked out and `build-perf` a no-op rebuild at the end; the
base worktree and venv left under `/workspace/output/round6/base/` (`git worktree prune` and
`rm -rf` remove them; they are 1.2 GB).
